  // columns of C per cache block (multiple of NR)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 128;       // depth of one k panel\n\n// Accumulate C[0..MR, 0..NR] += A[0..MR, 0..kc] * B[0..kc, 0..NR] with the\n// whole C tile held in vector registers. microKernel(const double* __restrict__ a, const double* __restrict__ b,\n                        double* __restrict__ c, const size_t N, const size_t kc) {\n    vecd acc0[MR];\n    vecd acc1[MR];\n i < MR; ++i) {\n            const double aik = ap[k * MR + i];\n            #pragma GCC unroll 4\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < PNRV; ++v) {\n                acc[i][v] += bv[v] * aik;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    #pragma GCC unroll 8\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        #pragma GCC unroll 4\n        for (size_t v = 0; v < PNRV; ++v) {\n            *reinterpret_cast<vecdu*>(c + i * ldc + v * VW) = acc[i][v];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy a kc x nBlk panel of B into NR-wide, k-major panels, zero padded.\ninline void packB(const double* __restrict__ Bp, double* __restrict__ dst, const size_t N,\n                  const size_t kBeg, const size_t kc, const size_t jBeg, const size_t nBlk) {\n    for (size_t q = 0; q * NR < nBlk; ++q) {\n        const size_t j0 = jBeg + q * NR;\n        const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nBlk - q * NR);\n        double* __restrict__ d = dst + q * NR * kc;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict__ src = Bp + (kBeg + k) * N + j0;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < nr; ++j) {\n                d[k * NR + j] = src[j];\n            }\n            for (size_t j = nr; j < NR; ++j) {\n                d[k * NR + j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy an mBlk x kc block of A into MR-tall, k-major panels, zero padded.\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict__ Ap, double* __restrict__ dst, const size_t N,\n                  const size_t kBeg, const size_t kc, const size_t iBeg, const size_t mBlk) {\n    for (size_t p = 0; p * MR < mBlk; ++p) {\n        const size_t i0 = iBeg + p * MR;\n        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mBlk - p * MR);\n        double* __restrict__ d = dst + p * MR * kc;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < mr; ++i) {\n            const double* __restrict__ src = Ap + (i0 + i) * N + kBeg;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                d[k * MR + i] = src[k];\n            }\n        }\n        for (size_t i = mr; i < MR; ++i) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                d[k * MR + i] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n} // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    const double* __restrict__ Ap = A.data();\n    const double* __restrict__ Bp = B.data();\n    double* __restrict__ Cp = C.data();\n\n    // Shrink the cache blocks if the default ones would not expose enough\n    // independent C blocks to keep every thread busy.\n    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    size_t mc = MC;\n    size_t nc = NC;\n    while ((N + mc - 1) / mc * ((N + nc - 1) / nc) < threads && (mc > MR || nc > NR)) {\n        if (mc * NR >= nc * MR && mc > MR) {\n            mc = std::max(MR, (mc / 2 + MR - 1) / MR * MR);\n        } else if (nc > NR) {\n            nc = std::max(NR, (nc / 2 + NR - 1) / NR * NR);\n        } else {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t iBlocks = (N + mc - 1) / mc;\n    const size_t jBlocks = (N + nc - 1) / nc;\n    const size_t kcMax = std::min(KC, N);\n    const size_t aPackSize = (mc + MR - 1) / MR * MR * kcMax;\n    const size_t bPackSize = (nc + NR - 1) / NR * NR * kcMax;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // C is overwritten (not accumulated) by the reference implementation,\n        // so every element has to start from zero before the k panels are added.\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            double* __restrict__ crow = Cp + i * N;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                crow[j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Packing buffers are private to each thread (and first touched by it,\n        // so they end up in the thread's local NUMA node).\n        std::vector<double> aPack(aPackSize);\n        std::vector<double> bPack(bPackSize);\n        double cTile[MR * NR];\n\n        // Each (ib, jb) block of C is owned by exactly one thread, and the k\n        // panels of a block are traversed in increasing order.\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n        for (size_t ib = 0; ib < iBlocks; ++ib) {\n            for (size_t jb = 0; jb < jBlocks; ++jb) {\n                const size_t iBeg = ib * mc;\n                const size_t mBlk = std::min(mc, N - iBeg);\n                const size_t jBeg = jb * nc;\n                const size_t nBlk = std::min(nc, N - jBeg);\n\n                for (size_t kBeg = 0; kBeg < N; kBeg += KC) {\n                    const size_t kc = std::min(KC, N - kBeg);\n\n                    packB(Bp, bPack.data(), N, kBeg, kc, jBeg, nBlk);\n                    packA(Ap, aPack.data(), N, kBeg, kc, iBeg, mBlk);\n\n                    for (size_t p = 0; p * MR < mBlk; ++p) {\n                        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mBlk - p * MR);\n                        const double* a = aPack.data() + p * MR * kc;\n\n                        for (size_t q = 0; q * NR < nBlk; ++q) {\n                            const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nBlk - q * NR);\n                            const double* b = bPack.data() + q * NR * kc;\n                            double* c = Cp + (iBeg + p * MR) * N + jBeg + q * NR;\n\n                            if (mr == MR && nr == NR) {\n                                microKernel(a, b, c, N, kc);\n                            } else {\n                                // Partial tile: work on a padded copy of C so\n                                // that the kernel may write full vectors.\n                                for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n                                    for (size_t j = 0; j < NR; ++j) {\n                                        cTile[i * NR + j] =\n                                            (i < mr && j < nr) ? c[i * N + j] : 0.0;\n                                    }\n                                }\n                                microKernel(a, b, cTile, NR, kc);\n                                for (size_t i = 0; i < mr; ++i) {\n                                    for (size_t j = 0; j < nr; ++j) {\n                                        c[i * N + j] = cTile[i * NR + j];\n                                    }\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\nopen('/tmp/tune.cpp','w').write(head+mid+tail)\nPY\ng++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 -fopenmp -o /tmp/tune /tmp/tune.cpp -I../common -Wall -Wextra 2>&1 | head -20; The register tile is MR x NR; per k panel a thread\n// keeps an MC x KC block of A and a KC x NC block of B in its private caches\n// while the MC x NC block of C it owns stays resident across all k panels.\nconstexpr size_t VW = MM_VECW;\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;       // rows of C per register tile\nconstexpr size_t NR = 2 * VW;  // columns of C per register tile\nconstexpr size_t MC = 256;     // rows of C per cache block\nconstexpr size_t NC = 256;     // columns of C per cache block\nconstexpr size_t KC = 64;      // depth of one k panel\n\n// Pin the OpenMP threads to distinct CPUs unless the runtime already binds them\n// (thread migration between the NUMA nodes costs more than half the throughput\n// on a multi socket machine). Only CPUs the process may run on are used.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num()) % cpus.size()], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\n// Drop the pages of a freshly allocated (and therefore zero filled by a single\n// thread) buffer, so that the parallel initialization below decides their NUMA\n// placement by first touch. Only whole pages inside the buffer are released,\n// and their contents stay zero, so this is invisible to the program.\nvoid releasePages(std::vector<double>& mat) {\n    const size_t page = static_cast<size_t>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));\n    const uintptr_t addr = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mat.data());\n    const uintptr_t beg = (addr + page - 1) & ~(static_cast<uintptr_t>(page) - 1);\n    const uintptr_t end = (addr + mat.size() * sizeof(double)) & ~(static_cast<uintptr_t>(page) - 1);\n    if (end > beg) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(beg), end - beg, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\nvoid initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {\n    releasePages(mat);\n\n    // Row blocked distribution: matches how the multiplication below splits the\n    // matrices, so every thread initializes the data it will work on.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nnamespace {\n\n// C[0..MR, 0..NR] += Apack[0..MR, 0..kc] * Bpack[0..kc, 0..NR] with the whole C\n// tile held in vector registers. Apack stores MR contiguous values per k, Bpack\n// NR contiguous values per k. The k iterations run in increasing order and\n// accumulate into the running value of C, exactly like the scalar reference\n// implementation does, so the results are bit identical to it.\ninline void microKernel(const double* __restrict__ aPack, const double* __restrict__ bPack,\n                        double* __restrict__ c, const size_t ldc, const size_t kc) {\n    vecd acc[MR][2];\n    #pragma GCC unroll 8\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        acc[i][0] = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(c + i * ldc);\n        acc[i][1] = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(c + i * ldc + VW);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const vecd b0 = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(bPack + k * NR);\n        const vecd b1 = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(bPack + k * NR + VW);\n        #pragma GCC unroll 8\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n            const double aik = aPack[k * MR + i];\n            acc[i][0] += b0 * aik;\n            acc[i][1] += b1 * aik;\n        }\n    }\n\n    #pragma GCC unroll 8\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        *reinterpret_cast<vecdu*>(c + i * ldc) = acc[i][0];\n        *reinterpret_cast<vecdu*>(c + i * ldc + VW) = acc[i][1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy a kc x nBlk block of B into NR wide, k major panels, zero padded.\ninline void packB(const double* __restrict__ Bp, double* __restrict__ dst, const size_t N,\n                  const size_t kBeg, const size_t kc, const size_t jBeg, const size_t nBlk) {\n    for (size_t q = 0; q * NR < nBlk; ++q) {\n        const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nBlk - q * NR);\n        double* __restrict__ panel = dst + q * NR * kc;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict__ src = Bp + (kBeg + k) * N + jBeg + q * NR;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < nr; ++j) {\n                panel[k * NR + j] = src[j];\n            }\n            for (size_t j = nr; j < NR; ++j) {\n                panel[k * NR + j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy an mBlk x kc block of A into MR tall, k major panels, zero padded.\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict__ Ap, double* __restrict__ dst, const size_t N,\n                  const size_t kBeg, const size_t kc, const size_t iBeg, const size_t mBlk) {\n    for (size_t p = 0; p * MR < mBlk; ++p) {\n        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mBlk - p * MR);\n        double* __restrict__ panel = dst + p * MR * kc;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < mr; ++i) {\n            const double* __restrict__ src = Ap + (iBeg + p * MR + i) * N + kBeg;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                panel[k * MR + i] = src[k];\n            }\n        }\n        for (size_t i = mr; i < MR; ++i) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n                panel[k * MR + i] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    const double* __restrict__ Ap = A.data();\n    const double* __restrict__ Bp = B.data();\n    double* __restrict__ Cp = C.data();\n\n    // Shrink the cache blocks if the default ones would not expose enough\n    // independent C blocks to keep every thread evenly busy.\n    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    size_t mc = MC;\n    size_t nc = NC;\n    while ((N + mc - 1) / mc * ((N + nc - 1) / nc) < 2 * threads && (mc > MR || nc > NR)) {\n        if (mc * NR >= nc * MR && mc > MR) {\n            mc = std::max(MR, (mc / 2 + MR - 1) / MR * MR);\n        } else if (nc > NR) {\n            nc = std::max(NR, (nc / 2 + NR - 1) / NR * NR);\n        } else {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t iBlocks = (N + mc - 1) / mc;\n    const size_t jBlocks = (N + nc - 1) / nc;\n    const size_t kcMax = std::min(KC, N);\n    const size_t aPackSize = (mc + MR - 1) / MR * MR * kcMax;\n    const size_t bPackSize = (nc + NR - 1) / NR * NR * kcMax;\n\n    // A single workspace for all threads; every thread first touches (and from\n    // then on only uses) its own slice. Allocating per thread inside the\n    // parallel region instead would cost more than the multiplication itself\n    // for small matrices, because every thread has to set up its own heap.\n    const size_t perThread = (aPackSize + bPackSize + NR - 1) / NR * NR;\n    std::unique_ptr<double[]> workspace(new double[threads * perThread + NR]);\n    double* const workspaceBase = workspace.get();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // C is overwritten (not accumulated) by the reference implementation,\n        // so every element has to start from zero before the k panels are added.\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t i = 0; The register tile is MR x NR; per k panel a thread\n// keeps an MC x KC block of A and a KC x NC block of B in its private caches\n// while the MC x NC block of C it owns stays resident across all k panels.\nconstexpr size_t VW = MM_VECW;\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;       // rows of C per register tile\nconstexpr size_t NR = 2 * VW;  // columns of C per register tile\nconstexpr size_t MC = 256;     // rows of C per cache block\nconstexpr size_t NC = 256;     // columns of C per cache block\nconstexpr size_t KC = 64;      // depth of one k panel\n\n// Pin the OpenMP threads to distinct CPUs unless the runtime already binds them\n// (thread migration between the NUMA nodes costs more than half the throughput\n// on a multi socket machine). Only CPUs the process may run on are used.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num()) % cpus.size()], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\n// Drop the pages of a freshly allocated (and therefore zero filled by a single\n// thread) buffer, so that the parallel initialization below decides their NUMA\n// placement by first touch. Only whole pages inside the buffer are released,\n// and their contents stay zero, so this is invisible to the program.\nvoid releasePages(std::vector<double>& mat) {\n    const size_t page = static_cast<size_t>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));\n    const uintptr_t addr = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mat.data());\n    const uintptr_t beg = (addr + page - 1) & ~(static_cast<uintptr_t>(page) - 1);\n    const uintptr_t end = (addr + mat.size() * sizeof(double)) & ~(static_cast<uintptr_t>(page) - 1);\n    if (end > beg) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(beg), end - beg, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\nvoid initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {\n    releasePages(mat);\n\n    // Row blocked distribution: matches how the multiplication below splits the\n    // matrices, so every thread initializes the data it will work on.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nnamespace {\n\n// C[0..MR, 0..NR] += Apack[0..MR, 0..kc] * Bpack[0..kc, 0..NR] with the whole C\n// tile held in vector registers. Apack stores MR contiguous values per k, Bpack\n// NR contiguous values per k. The k iterations run in increasing order and\n// accumulate into the running value of C, exactly like the scalar reference\n// implementation does, so the results are bit identical to it.\ninline void microKernel(const double* __restrict__ aPack, const double* __restrict__ bPack,\n                        double* __restrict__ c, const size_t ldc, const size_t kc) {\n    vecd acc[MR][2];\n    #pragma GCC unroll 8\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        acc[i][0] = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(c + i * ldc);\n        acc[i][1] = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(c + i * ldc + VW);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const vecd b0 = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(bPack + k * NR);\n        const vecd b1 = *reinterpret_cast<const vecdu*>(bPack + k * NR + VW);\n        #pragma GCC unroll 8\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n            const double aik = aPack[k * MR + i];\n            acc[i][0] += b0 * aik;\n            acc[i][1] += b1 * aik;\n        }\n    }\n\n    #pragma GCC unroll 8\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < MR; ++i) {\n        *reinterpret_cast<vecdu*>(c + i * ldc) = acc[i][0];\n        *reinterpret_cast<vecdu*>(c + i * ldc + VW) = acc[i][1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy a kc x nBlk block of B into NR wide, k major panels, zero padded.\ninline void packB(const double* __restrict__ Bp, double* __restrict__ dst, const size_t N,\n                  const size_t kBeg, const size_t kc, const size_t jBeg, const size_t nBlk) {\n    for (size_t q = 0; q * NR < nBlk; ++q) {\n        const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nBlk - q * NR);\n        double* __restrict__ panel = dst + q * NR * kc;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict__ src = Bp + (kBeg + k) * N + jBeg + q * NR;\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < nr; ++j) {\n                panel[k * NR + j] = src[j];\n            }\n            for (size_t j = nr; j < NR; ++j) {\n                panel[k * NR + j] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy an mBlk x kc block of A into MR tall, k major panels, zero padded.\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict__ Ap, double* __restrict__ dst, const size_t N,\n                  const size_t kBeg, const size_t kc, const size_t iBeg, const size_t mBlk) {\n    for (size_t p = 0; Allocating per thread inside the\n    // parallel region instead would cost more than the multiplication itself\n    // for small matrices, because every thread has to set up its own heap.\n    const                    }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cek1kQFG11muAdDFF68Yw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` now contain an unconditionally OpenMP-parallel, cache-blocked matmul.\n\n**What the implementation does**\n\n- **Blocked, packed GEMM**: each thread owns a distinct `MC×NC` block of C (`collapse(2)` over the block grid, static schedule) and walks it k-panel by k-panel. A and B panels are packed into per-thread buffers (`MR`-major / `NR`-major, zero-padded) so the kernel reads them contiguously.\n- **Register micro-kernel** (`6×2·VW`): the whole C tile stays in vector registers across the k loop; written with GCC vector extensions, so the width follows the target (AVX-512 → 8, AVX2 → 4, else SSE2 → 2). The generated inner loop is 12 vector mul/adds + 8 loads, no spills.\n- **NUMA first touch**: `madvise(MADV_DONTNEED)` drops the pages the `std::vector` constructor faulted in single-threaded, then the parallel `initMatrix` (and a parallel zeroing of C in `main`, both outside the timed region) place them on the node of the thread that will use them.\n- **Thread pinning**: `bindThreads()` self-pins threads to distinct CPUs from the process affinity mask, but only when the runtime isn't binding already (`omp_get_proc_bind() == omp_proc_bind_false`), so `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`taskset` still win.\n- **One shared workspace** allocated before the parallel region instead of per-thread buffers inside it — per-thread heap setup alone cost ~35 ms at 128 threads.\n\n**Correctness**: the k accumulation order is unchanged (ascending, accumulating into C), and `-ffp-contract=off` was added to the compile options so the kernel performs the same multiply-then-add sequence as the original. Result hashes are bit-identical to the original binary for N = 512, 1000, 1024, 2048; odd N differ by 1 ulp only because the original's own unrolled loop uses an FMA for its leftover iteration. `-v` validation passes for N = 1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 13, 63, 127, 333, 512, 1024, 2048.\n\n**Performance** (dual EPYC 7763, 128 cores / 256 threads):\n\n| N | original (1 thread) | new (all threads) |\n|---|---|---|\n| 512 | 691 ms / 0.39 GFLOPS | 4 ms / 67 GFLOPS |\n| 4096 | ~0.3 GFLOPS | 61 ms / 2253 GFLOPS |\n| 8192 | — | 2569 GFLOPS |\n\nThread scaling at N=4096: 21.5 / 40.6 / 81.5 / 164 / 329 / 654 / 1249 / 2253 GFLOPS for 1…128 threads (~105× on 128 cores); 256 threads (SMT) helps at N=8192 but is slightly slower at N=4096."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":368,"cache_read_input_tokens":142443,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":368},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"75cbb2e9-d520-445d-9706-acdac78db8a5","uuid":"c54e530f-a172-4084-bb13-d60edcf2b916","timestamp":"2026-09-05T08:56:33.563Z","request_id":"req_011Cek1kPLBbXnrtgZSW376i"}
